Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cohomologies variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 338 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 553 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 338 + O(1/n^553). Summing over all modes and applying the cohomologies convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
